#### Complete original discovery lineage register

All 289 original uncoded discovery descriptions, source bounds, eligibility and fixed-unit intersections remain reproduced verbatim in the [Humor Mechanics Ledger’s complete discovery register](../03%20Longitudinal%20Ledgers/KONOSUBA_HUMOR_MECHANICS_LEDGER.md#complete-original-discovery-lineage-register). That canonical longitudinal home preserves every original and its scoped mapping. This reading retains all 150 complete paired operative coding rows, the conservative census, all external qualifications and all 95 paired voice records below. The duplicate 83,164-byte discovery-register block was replaced only by this route to satisfy the repository’s 1 MiB per-file review limit; no discovery, code, rationale, boundary or history was removed from the published analytical transaction.

## 8. Japanese written stance, role and constrained comparison

Written stance changes with addressee, audience, role and access to agency. Kazuma’s prose can call Serena 「様」 while his spoken position still resists a particular cost, and the post-cure narrator can change address before he can explain why. Aqua’s polite first-person divine persona can issue practical instructions, concede limited ability, attack a rival or ask a quiet personal question. Darkness’s noble intervention, personal objection and bodily fantasy require different semantic contexts even when the grammar overlaps. Megumin’s polite forms do not weaken her competitive claim or the force of her relational request. None of this warrants an acoustic description of actors, pitch, accent, timing or loudness beyond what the written narration explicitly reports.

The fixed union retains95 original records:85 primary-four comparisons and10 auxiliary observations, with190 before/after arrays. The66 overlap presentation groups are display groupings rather than independent tests. Each original mapping, speaker/addressee description, role cue, surface exclusion, limit and exact boundary remains immutable. Shared precode factual corrections are external qualifications. The producer’s complete separate formal assignments and the all 25 provisional memo were sealed before any other-reader formal outcomes or closed predicate notes were opened. This preserves separate discovery and assignment histories, not total blindness: the lineages share a native source, collaborated on the uncoded union and received bounded factual/scope QA before coding.

| Source / speaker → addressee and context | Short exact material form and literal value | Pragmatic account and scope |
|---|---|---|

| C1P0013–0018, Kazuma → Serena privately; Dust is a narrated comparison, not present | 「持ってないっス」 means “I do not have any” in a clipped colloquially polite form. | His clipped deference follows Serena’s rough cigarette demand and Kazuma’s intimidated self-appraisal. P17–18 contrasts his ease with Dust; it is self-regarding stance, not adopted care or a universal politeness rule. |
| C1P0468–0486, Kazuma → Aqua, Darkness and Megumin after Aqua’s vulnerability | 「頼む」, request/entrust, has specific practical objects in the assigned tasks. | Support, healing, police transport and crowd handoffs redirect action. The vulnerability question’s role-declaring content cannot also become its own successful form evidence. |

Actual words, quoted interior and narration must not be pooled as one speech register. |

Serena’s incredulity cannot establish Kazuma’s theology as an exact census or make plural practice a single exclusive faith. |

The same text records power resistance and a pragmatic violation, not a successful order merely because the imperative is clear. |

Erasure motive is a narrator inference; the surviving text does not show who traveled with her or completed rescue. |

Literal glosses identify the selected fragment only; pragmatic descriptions use the actual surrounding source. A short form may have multiple functions without becoming evidence of every function. Role-declaring content is excluded from successful form evidence when it alone supplies the trigger. Exact source member and paragraph locators remain attached to the complete95-row private assignment file; final public presentation should preserve short anchors, literal/pragmatic distinction, addressees and every original limit while omitting full private paragraph arrays.

The provisional whole J01 result remains PARTIAL. Some care, duty, dignity, specialist and relational directions have bounded support; sparse speech, active-role baselines, adjacent subtypes and opposite directions remain. The initial full assignments preserve adverse Aqua self-legitimation cases and the Kazuma mapping mismatch rather than remap them to whatever form appears. The auxiliary Serena/Vanir/Wiz/Eris/Yunyun observations are valuable contrasts but are not primary-four J01 trials. They retain their original descriptive status. At the initial draft seal other-reader comparison was still closed; the complete paired comparison has now been received and is included below, with both original rationales, all limits and external factual corrections. No original row is silently recoded.

J01’s independently established dignity threat does not universally require D02’s separate “otherwise sought bounded event” and prior matching privacy-preference conjunction. The independent reader’s privacy qualifications in the original voice records remain its attributable reasoning about test strength, not newly imposed universal J01 prerequisites. Actual externally imposed false blame or intimate public disclosure can independently establish a local dignity stake, while prior personal objection and sparse speech can still limit new-onset evidence.

The original IV15-24 judgments retain a substantive direction disagreement: the producer reads Aqua’s 「私以下」 as comparative self-legitimation; the independent reader distinguishes relative disparagement from the narrower divine-legitimacy/acknowledgment function and retains an adverse-direction reading with sparse/already-active limits. Other supported/partial differences concern adequate independent role cues, already active stance and exact subtype. No label is changed to manufacture consensus. “Written” here describes the textually represented utterances and inter-world calls; it does not imply diegetic correspondence where characters are speaking.

### MAIN-15 complete paired written-voice registry — candidate

All 95 exact original comparisons are retained: 85 primary-four records and 10 auxiliary records. Original origin IDs, roles, mappings, addressees, before/after bounds, exclusions and weak limits remain fixed. The 66 mechanical same-speaker after-overlap groups are presentation aids, **not 66 or 95 independent tests**. Several rows are parallel views of the same source event, sometimes under different fixed mappings.

P and I below are the two separately sealed initial evaluations of each exact record, not the origin of the window. Both initial disposition/rationale/limits are retained. P’s SUPPORTED/ROLE_LIMITED/PARTIAL/ADVERSE/NOT_TESTED/AUXILIARY and I’s more granular labels are not silently normalized into agreement; differences in observed function, eligibility, prior role activity, speech adequacy and role/form circularity remain distinct. A local functional fit does not by itself establish a newly adopted role. An already active or sparse baseline is not repaired by replacing a window.

These are written principal-source observations. No acoustic, recorded-prosody, human-review, independent-trial, frequency-rate or all-25 forecast result is claimed. In particular, any retaliation is not automatically divine self-legitimation, one speaker’s frozen map is not transferred to another, and role-defining semantics cannot validate their own form when they are the sole role cue. The J01 specialist mapping and the separate M01 literal forecast trigger must not be conflated or silently strengthened by a universal named-rival-spell/debate requirement.

All locators use the same principal-member/all-body-p convention as the humor appendix. The short exact forms are a postcoding illustrative selection within each unchanged fixed side; no full private source window or ruby XML is reproduced. “No selected illustration” means none was chosen for that side, not necessarily no speech; the paired rationale specifies true missing-speech cases. For PA10, Vanir supplies the before form and Aqua supplies the copied after form. Other displayed forms belong to the row’s target speaker unless an explicit narrative/written layer is named in the original context. External notes govern corrected source presentation, without erasing the original wording.

## 9. Kazuma’s narrator grammar and knowledge limits

Kazuma is a selective, defensive focalizer whose reports still contain useful self-correction, causal testing and inconvenient evidence about himself. The volume does not warrant global factual unreliability. It does require tracking the difference between a visible event, an attributed report, a private goal, an interpretive gloss and a retrospective explanation. A comic phrase can be accurate but hostile; a useful inference can remain uncertain; a later confirmation cannot create earlier knowledge.

| Evidence route / native source | What is established | What the narration cannot establish by itself |
|---|---|---|
| Creature fight, PrP0027–0038 | Aqua reports fire after Kazuma chooses not to look at that instant; he recalls prior fire behavior and admits uncertain identity. | An independently witnessed present fire emission by the focalizer, or the creature’s true species. Aqua’s no-demon/soap smell statement is a bounded report whose accuracy is not independently certified. |
| Officer admissions, C1P0034–0043 | Serena now claims corpse staging and a reusable fabricated maiden story. | That the V14 original reader knew the method; a full independent history of every previous use. |
| Legend versus contribution, C1P0066–0126/P0143–0153 | He enjoys credit, fears a cosmic explanation, exaggerates willpower, then privately credits the team and his own prior worsening of a foe. | A universal actual rank as strongest hero, or that every self-deprecating thought is equally false. |
| Guild ban/bill, C1P0167–0170 | Companions report exclusion and a bill; he infers the threatened alcohol purification caused it. | A newly directly witnessed transformation or exact event sequence in the guild. |
| Patient, C1P0313–0363 | Access block, repeated Serena healing, residual wounds, rejected Aqua hand and changed reverence are observable narrative events. | A failed Aqua cast, complete Serena treatment or certified puppetry mechanism before the expressly tentative hypothesis. |
| Motive denial, C1P0350–0385; C2P0715–0723 | Repeated concern and initial revenge admission coexist with later insistence Aqua was irrelevant. | That the denial cancels earlier attachment or that every helping act had only one pure motive. |
| Silent companions, C1P0427–0439 | Physical touches and silence are shown. | Exact hidden knowledge or full consent to the concealed bargain; his interpretation uses explicit possibility. |
| Police aftermath, C2P0035–0038/P0073 | Incapacitation and present lack of wounds are narrated; absence of a complaint is reported by the chief. | Direct observation of the unconscious healing interval or an illegal release inferred solely from discretion. |
| Royal response, C2P0434–0447 | Letter contents are his report; a joint guarantee, Rain’s rushed appearance and Claire’s retreat are officer/staff reports. | A newly witnessed Claire/Iris scene, actual romance or certain exact mental states of the guarantors. |
| Follower transitions, C2P0610–0638 | Serena takes a follower’s money to repay Kazuma, after which brief lucidity, skirt display/thanks and renewed vacancy are observed. | His inferred debt rule as omniscient universal law before explicit later explanation; why Serena originally chose the display method. |
| Captured consciousness, C2P0757–0815/P0824–0834 | First-person motor/cognitive impairment and the external restraint-discarding order change access and allegiance. | A later voluntary resistance scheme absent from the current interior, or complete loss of every initiative. Narrator address itself changes, but that is not automatically speech. |
| Diary, C3P0006–0118 | The altered narrator’s dated claims and valuations are presented as a document; his erotic passage P29 is marked invented fiction. | Actual naked Serena contact/words from that fictional passage; unqualified direct witnessing of each reported prior act. |
| Shared faith, C3P0131–0175 | Mirrored test injury and attributed doctrine establish a local problem; native ruby reads 八百万 as やおよろず. | A literal exact eight-million-god census or interchangeable definitions of 無宗派/無宗教. |
| Apparition, C3P0209–0218 | He thinks he saw a blond girl and feels nostalgic attachment; Serena suspects ghost perception. | The girl’s independently confirmed identity or certainty that every perception is false. |
| Cure state change, C3P0443–0475 | Felt release, changed address, brief revenge recollection P456, then inability to explain at P471–475. | An invented continuous stable memory state or certain deliberate lying to reconcile the two access points. |
| Solo completion, C3P0508 | He frames the selected reset as finishing alone. | Erasure of actual capture, cure, restraint, supplied item or later resurrection work by others. |
| Death room, C4P0017/P0089–0137 | Direct conversation with Eris; her report of ground aftermath; Aqua-mediated body-interference calls; later guilty behavior and disordered trousers. | Eris’s observation mechanism, the complete threatened act or its completion merely from suggestive reports. The edible object is unnamed here. |
| Institutional aftermath, C4P0156–0165/P0324 | Reset and police/intelligence handoff reported as completed; countermeasures/bounty and royal transfer have different prospective/report status. | Completed payment, completed transfer or a shown cancelled battle. |
| Other minds, C4P0283–0292/P0511; FP0151/P0354 | He explicitly entertains alternatives for Aqua, infers why Yunyun is pleased, reads Vanir’s step aside and suspects reciprocal guilt. | Omniscient private motive. Visible expressions/actions can support a qualified interpretation without proving it uniquely. |
| Routine predictions, FP0207–0208/P0369–0370 | Hunger prediction fails by nightfall; later planning does end in sleep. | All-night confinement or indefinite insomnia after the balcony. |
| Letter, EpP0025–0065 | Aqua’s allegations, inflated faith, declared goal and erased please-find-me text are read. | Newly staged laundry conduct, audited follower count, observed writing/erasure, confirmed solitary travel, actual hired escort or the pursuers’ completed journey. |

Several repeated rhetorical processes are visible. He gives self-interest civic language when accepting safety and petty violence a justice label when defending arrest (C1P0131–0132; C2P0484). He adds 「様」 under changed allegiance and later wonders why, so the narrator’s naming must not be pooled indiscriminately as timeless spoken politeness (C2P0824; C3P0451–0453). He appreciates another’s distress under control, then after cure finds the resulting allegiance dreamlike; affect and access have changed, not merely the vocabulary of one unchanged rational actor. He can also narrate an unattractive actual thought—abandonment, murder, a household without Aqua—then explicitly decline or revise it. Suppressing those thoughts would make the model less accurate; treating them as completed choices would do the same.

His frame is often accurate in detail and misleading in total credit. He really makes useful decisions and accepts costs, yet claims solitary completion after depending on the team. He really has enough money and a low level ceiling to recover, yet the reset is still an actual cost rather than a free performance. He really recognizes moonlit beauty while speaking through grievances and naps. He accurately notices a possible strategy while rejecting a guaranteed personal victory. The source’s own competing evidence constrains a narrower account than either a perfect hidden mastermind or a narrator whose whole world can be discarded as exaggeration.

## 10. Seriousness overrides and failures to override

A serious scene is a change in the active value hierarchy, not a guarantee that all comic habits stop. V15 has several genuine interruptions and several continuations in the presence of distress. The comparison must preserve who is threatened, how the focalizer knows, what state the actor is in and whether a different action was actually chosen.

| Trigger / source | Prior script and observed change | What remains unresolved or adverse |
|---|---|---|
| Aqua’s unusual exclusion, C1P0350–0486 | Kazuma’s safety/comfort bargain becomes difficult to tolerate; he rejects uncertain shortcuts and accepts an anticipated injury/legal burden to intervene. | Care already exists before the last plea. Actual method is assault, intimidation and later humiliating harassment, not ethically purified rescue. |
| Trusted silence, C2P0064–0085 | Darkness’s expected strictness yields a request for discretionary release and offer of aftermath support. | No complaint exists; institutional responsibility and particular loyalty conflict. His later humiliation can still end her willingness to repeat the favor. |
| Darkness’s public exposure, C2P0096–0115/P0373 | Shame produces an explicit no-more-guarantees boundary, later actually invoked. | Continued affection and Kazuma’s mild-anger reading do not erase that applied limit. No prior universal privacy preference is invented. |
| Serena’s tears under domination, C3 diary/P0167–0181/P0286 | The altered Kazuma often treats her distress as attractive, applies payment logic and continues coercion despite tears/protests. | Imposed altered allegiance/restraint loss is a major state qualification. It cannot make the actual actions or tears disappear, nor be used as a new unannounced immunity from adverse evidence. |
| Imminent Death at home, C3P0341–0351 | Darkness interposes before the spell, Aqua/Darkness capture the weakened caster and routine bargaining stops. | The failed spell’s explanation comes later. Protective action need not mean the group already knows every rule. |
| Bottle loss, C3P0425–0443 | Initial cure confidence fails; Aqua immediately preserves the bottle, then all participants tighten the application strategy. | Recovery is local and cooperative, not proof there was never a failure or that one actor was flawless. |
| Murder proposal, C3P0480–0520 | Kazuma’s reluctance/attachment and Darkness’s civic killing offer produce a chosen nonlethal reset with expected personal cost. | Affordable asymmetric loss remains part of his appraisal. Darkness’s later pinching still exploits a captive’s pain; public duty does not uniformly dominate pleasure. |
| Repeated death, C4P0023–0088 | Genuine demoralization and desire for a different life persist despite Eris’s comfort and Aqua’s calls. | Teasing and a rich-pet fantasy coexist with real reluctance. A later provocative body threat, rather than the first rational appeal, changes his immediate behavior. |
| Body-interference calls, C4P0089–0122 | Sexual opportunity reading becomes bodily threat; Kazuma abandons refusal and urgently returns. | Full degree of interference is unseen; the response primarily protects his own body, and world information competes unsuccessfully for attention. |
| Aqua’s tears after mud, C4P0425–0441 | Kazuma selects another material after water fails as deterrence and leaves satisfied after actually making her cry. | This is outside the puppet episode and adverse to any broad automatic distress brake. Exact frozen branch antecedents still need their own literal local assessment; no blanket exclusion for a running gag. |
| Yunyun’s conflicting duty, C4P0483–0520 | A real wish for town friends meets future-chief obligation; Megumin provides forceful support through destroying the document. | Specialist resentment, known document externality and the narrator’s motive inference remain. Support can change a friend’s feasible story while serving the helper’s status. |
| Wiz’s chosen acceptance, FP0136–0168 | Expected opposition becomes consent, gratitude and love; Aqua retreats and never casts. | Narrator-labelled conscience and the observers’ gaze both matter. Raw ability is not tested by nonexecution; the scene cannot isolate pure private altruism or establish a universal response to praise. |
| Aqua absent at nightfall, FP0172–0244 | Food delivery fails to restore routine, and Kazuma’s ordinary sleep is interrupted by unwanted attachment thoughts. | Counterfactual material benefits briefly appeal. Later he does fall asleep P369–370; genuine concern is not endless insomnia or a chosen efficient separation. |
| Quiet home wish, FP0307–0354 | His lecture/grievance frame yields to recognition of an unknown prior life, responsibility and a limited constructive offer; reciprocal no-regret exchange follows. | Actual mission/return is not completed; teasing/threats continue. Her exact reciprocal guilt remains his inference, not confession. |
| Departure letter, EpP0047–0065 | Bedbound leisure becomes actual standing/jumping and immediate pursuit intention. | No onstage attack, actual solitude, route or completed pursuit yet. The only short spoken exclamation is not an invented handoff command. |

The strongest model constraints therefore include both selected stops and non-stops. Aqua’s attempted cleansing stops when its recipient’s trust makes it unbearable; Kazuma’s post-mud satisfaction remains despite tears. Darkness’s love for a companion can bend official behavior and create a personal boundary, while her enjoyment of reflected pain can still impose harm on an enemy. Megumin can use intimacy in a genuine cure and withdraw it the moment his pretext exceeds the situation. Seriousness is local, sometimes temporary and often relationship-specific; it does not convert the cast into an idealized version of itself.

## 11. Ordinary life and preferences

The novel makes ordinary comfort the object at risk, so mundane evidence is part of its causal structure. A one-off behavior, a reported habit, a state-amplified appetite and a repeatedly observed preference should remain distinguishable.

| Actor / domain | Source-bound observation | Scope and limit |
|---|---|---|
| Kazuma and Aqua: meals | Late shared breakfast, fish, soy sauce and salt structure PrP0004–0019. Kazuma wants to eat without being shaken P0031; Aqua wants edible seasoning rather than water alone. | Basic local preferences, not a ranked cuisine model or evidence every meal is late. |
| Kazuma: sleep and work | Requests sleeping until evening after a reported late victory drinking session C4P0153/P0167; treats the delayed escort assignment as legitimate leisure P0366–0368; EpP0020 reveals noon. | Repeated comfort/laziness tendency, with actual sleepless concern FP0212 and later actual sleep FP0369–0370 as contextual variation. |
| Kazuma: spending/resources | Money supports mana stones, express delivery and an affordable rebuild C2P0243–0244/P0423; C3P0532. Hoarded supplies evoke his game-player habit C4P0329–0332. | Stated hoarding is not literal proof he never uses an item; recent actual reset is an obvious counterexample to an unlimited claim. |
| Kazuma: luxurious meals under control | Diary contrasts sukiyaki/barbecue with Serena’s deprivation C3P0052–0075 and records her cost of his lodging/luxury. | Altered restraint/allegiance amplifies appetite and entitlement; do not pool state-specific coercion as a stable unconditional ordinary dinner script. |
| Kazuma: desirable company | Imagines wealthy life with Megumin/Darkness, then recognizes boredom without Aqua FP0235–0244; admires moonlit Aqua privately FP0266–0287. | Actual counterfactual and private appraisals, not a selected household reorganization, new love declaration or elimination of stated nonromantic distinctions. |
| Kazuma: privacy and embarrassment | Concealment avoids a feared nocturnal-visit interpretation FP0251–0257; dream-service secrecy is exploitable by Vanir C2P0639–0640. | Known vulnerabilities recruit compliance/furtiveness. No actual sexual encounter with each dream figure or actual accusation at night is shown. |
| Aqua: food and withdrawal | She requests delivered food outside FP0200–0202, despite refusing company; has not emerged by nightfall P0208. | Food appetite remains while familiar social routine changes; not all-night room confinement and not proof the later quiet mood was fake. |
| Aqua: pets | Shares food poorly with Chomusuke but treats Zel as a child, insists it is a dragon, feeds the level potion and negotiates its coop C4P0369–0420. | Actual attachment and unilateral resource use coexist with imaginative narration of clucks; species and future combat utility unproved. |
| Aqua and Darkness: hygiene/labor | Mud leads Aqua to bathe while Darkness washes the robe C4P0444. | Actual local allocation; no general promise that the same person always repairs every conflict. |
| Aqua: leisure/exhibitions | Reportedly detains both companions through a half-day performance C2P0494; Kazuma still wants to see it P0497. | Reported duration/art, not independently observed full performance or measured effect. Curiosity does not imply the hazardous sleepover is real leisure. |
| Aqua: saving and departure | Darkness expects savings from recent allowances to finance the departure and hypothesizes hired adventurers EpP0052. | Attributed financial expectation, no amount audited and no actual escort purchase shown. Do not infer global thrift from one goal-linked report. |
| Darkness: clothing | Date-like dress, long gloves and parasol replace armor for the supposed outing C1P0191–0195; public teasing is unwanted P0258–0264. | Prepared special occasion, not a stable dislike of armor or consent to display. |
| Darkness and Megumin: leisure | Actually play a board game at home FP0173; Kazuma guesses Megumin is ahead from their expressions P0206. | Shared ordinary leisure is stronger evidence than the guessed rank. No game type or durable skill hierarchy is supplied. |
| Megumin: domestic provision | Kazuma receives and carries her prepared meal FP0179–0182. | Local practical care; cooking process not directly watched and no general culinary ranking follows. |
| Megumin: familiar practice | Housebound upward Explosion and repeated arrest are reported C2P0264. | Recurrent daily preference/constraint, with legal/external cost retained; no unshown injuries or safe universal ritual inferred. |
| Yunyun: company | Requests a supper friend, later has concrete town contacts and wants to defend them C1P0219; C4P0483–0493. | Development in actual social stakes, not a timeless absolute absence of friends. Kazuma’s unfavorable labels are attributable. |
| Serena: smoking | Requests tobacco after suppressing the habit for the saint persona C1P0012; later actually brings out pipe-like equipment and smokes C2P0735–0747. | Two relevant contexts support a local habit, not product composition, health history or an invented real-world tobacco equivalence. |
| Serena: ordinary work constraints | The diary reports that her card reveals the dark-priest occupation when accepting adventurer requests and links this to missing guild rewards C3P0067–0068; paid consultation, home piecework and dishwashing are described. | Institutional constraint explains local income difficulty; no generic inability to work or objective endorsement of the narrator’s dependency claims. |
| Wiz: shop/home | Buys cookies with merchandise FP0120, calls the shop a return-home place for former companions P0152 and values the past year P0148. | Chosen ordinary hospitality and belonging help explain consent; no closed side narrative is opened to identify every friend. |
| Vanir: project/merchandise | Dungeon agreement motivates commercial labor by his own declaration FP0142; secret body pillow is a proposed product FP0113. | Goal is reported and actual protection corroborates attachment, but no completed product sale, consent from the depicted shopkeeper or finished dungeon is shown. |

The ordinary-life counterfactuals constrain motivation without deciding it alone. Money makes a reset affordable but does not make losing levels costless. A mansion makes separation materially feasible but does not make Aqua dispensable. Food remains wanted during shame but fails to restore company by a guessed time. A pet can receive devoted care and still become an excuse for unilateral expenditure. These are usable state-dependent preferences, with the surrounding inconvenience and labor left visible.

## 12. Worldbuilding, institutions and genre dependencies

The world makes recognition causally dangerous, but the novel does not reduce every bond to magical debt. Serena describes a system in which an acknowledged favor permits control, concentration on one exceptionally devoted believer consumes her available allocation, and shared faith changes reflected injury. Observed transitions partly support that account: Serena takes a follower's money to repay Kazuma, after which he briefly regains lucidity; her new displayed favor and his thanks precede renewed vacancy (C2P0602–0638); Kazuma's thanks precedes motor/cognitive impairment (P0745–0807); shared-faith injury appears in the toothpick test (C3P0131–0139); attempted corpse puppetry fails while the control allocation remains committed to him (P0271–0286); removal of his faith precedes Serena's renewed lethal spell (P0538–0548). Those routes differ in access. The doctrine is interested testimony; Puppet points is Kazuma's label, not an independently displayed game interface; one local sequence is not a general law covering every god or every grateful person.

The system's comic failure follows from the gap between controlling allegiance and specifying preferences. A servant can value the master's cause yet make help expensive, demand compensation, resist release and introduce new obligations. Serena's command to discard conscience and restraint changes the cost of his existing appetites; it does not supply each later idea. Her inability to cheaply dismiss him is both a fictional rule and a social-modeling failure. In a different world's reconstruction, that precise divine allocation and automatic injury reflection would require replacement; bargaining ingenuity, self-serving accounting and the difference between loyalty and useful compliance can remain psychologically intelligible.

Occupational infrastructure makes identity materially consequential. Megumin's early reward/card inference is initially a hypothesis (C1P0176); the diary later reports that Serena cannot accept adventurer requests without revealing the dark-priest occupation on her card and links this to the missing guild rewards (C3P0067–0068). Piecework, dishwashing, consultation and debt are therefore more than background jokes. The reported card constraint restricts that adventurer/guild income route while a dependent consumes resources. It establishes no general legal ban on ordinary paid work; piecework and dishwashing remain actual alternatives. Nor does it certify every complaint in the altered narrator's diary as an impartial account. The beginner town similarly remains a locally protected training environment in Serena's explanation, while Kazuma recalls his extraordinary experience there (C2P0796–0801). Neither perspective alone measures the ordinary resident's risk.

Police and noble institutions are neither uniformly effective nor irrelevant. The first release includes a reported absence of a formal complaint, Darkness's trusted-person discretion and her physical unlocking (C2P0064–0085). Her later refusal creates an actual constraint; the express royal guarantee changes the police's response again (P0373–0382/P0423–0447). The women providing that guarantee are not directly staged in this volume's corresponding royal scene. Eventually the reset captive and information reach the police (C4P0156–0157), but countermeasures, bounty and a later royal transfer retain their separate promise/planning status (P0158–0165/P0323–0324). Familiar comic corruption cannot turn each reported future event into a completed administrative fact.

The defense summons links local friendship, village obligation and national danger without making them the same task. Yunyun wants to help actual town contacts, while her future-chief role makes a rare call difficult to ignore (C4P0483–0493). The document classifies Megumin as reserve; Yunyun infers that her inability to use advanced magic is probably the reason (P0473–0474). The listing and her hedged explanation have distinct authority. The classification wounds Megumin's specialist standing but is not automatically explicit denigration, replacement or imitation of Explosion under the frozen M01 trigger. Its destruction carries a known shared-document cost because the other recipients' need is stated before the blast (P0476/P0498–0501). An offered excuse and mixed pleased response follow; actual release from duty, completed stay and successful town defense remain unobserved. The joke preserves an institutional externality even when a friend benefits.

Afterlife familiarity does not remove death's motivational weight. Kazuma can appreciate Eris's playfulness and still express fatigue, fear and a desire for another life (C4P0006–0088). Aqua's resurrection offers a real contingency with a body-destruction limit later explicitly stated (FP0324); it is not proof that any harm is harmless or reversible without cost. The body-threat persuasion makes his immediate personal danger more salient than a world-level message, and the message must later be reconstructed. Eris's report about an honest replacement recruiter establishes a bounded institutional effectiveness problem (C4P0176–0188). It does not establish that honesty always fails or morally vindicate every misleading recruitment method.

Finally, practical value depends on context even when fantasy ability is real. The weather charm's strong magic is compatible with negligible local seasonal use according to Vanir's weather account (FP0081–0114). Aqua's empty-castle idea has a recognizable strategic rationale without verified future guard behavior (C4P0393–0409). A rooster's item-induced growth does not certify dragon species or combat capacity (P0417–0423). These distinctions matter in transfer: preserve the character's selected constraint, available information and reward; recalibrate the world's mechanisms and consequences instead of simply removing every comic choice.

Genre scripts also supply explicit comparison standards: blue-tanuki problem solving, Spelunker fragility, final-boss consumable hoarding, sister wake-up and maid reception make the cast’s own expectations legible. Their cultural recognition and Japanese written forms receive separate treatment in sections 7–8. The social script never proves actual capacity: wishing for effortless solutions, recalling an unusually fragile game protagonist or naming a heroine does not establish a new spell, fixed psychology or a successful campaign. The source can make an act comic while preserving the death, resource loss, refusal and household disruption it causes.

## 13. Longitudinal model implications and claim transitions

These are V15 evidence transitions, not a replacement for the frozen Gen04 model, a formal V13–V15 closeout, or a Gen05 freeze. Gen04's original text and all 25 original blank field pairs remain exact. Section14 must separately assess every forecast under its original conjunctions and failure criteria. Earlier V13 K03, V14 A02 and Gen03 K04 adverse histories remain controlling observations at their own boundaries.

| Subject / claim | Transition and source route | Consequence and uncertainty |
|---|---|---|
| Kazuma: bounded causal search and revision | STRENGTHEN: the cup shot reveals indirect reflection, and he abandons the contemplated explosive-potion method C2P0200–0214. Earlier curse/bind/consultation alternatives are weighed C1P0395–0415; later a reset supplies a nonlethal route C3P0502–0515. | A specific invalidating fact can change the selected method. Clever search does not entail good ethics, sustained self-monitoring or safe extraction; capture and Death remain actual failures. |
| Kazuma: reward and pursuit | OPEN / retain contrary evidence: concrete concession and privately acknowledged revenge are followed by reconciliation C2P0705–0733. | Compare the fixed prior scope and later decision. Prior harm to Wiz P0700 and original danger/relationship modifiers remain; goal completion is not an added exemption from Gen04. The V13 unchanged-scope failure cannot be rescued by V14's distinct positive case. |
| Kazuma: agency under domination | OPEN a new state-specific decomposition beyond the inadequate normal-agent/empty-puppet alternatives: impaired motor control, imposed loss of restraints and changed allegiance C2P0757–0834 coexist with initiative, calculation and painful refusal C3P0006–0286. | Conditional state model: preserve both coercion and retained choices. No secret conscious infiltration plan is established. Post-cure dreamlike recall and later inability to explain are distinct access points C3P0451–0475. |
| Kazuma: relational care and desire | STRENGTHEN particular-person attachment; OPEN its triggers: concern C1P0350–0385, food delivery FP0172–0202, embarrassed absence thoughts P0212–0257, responsibility and limited offer P0307–0322, sincere no-regret reply P0342–0354. | Care does not guarantee kindness, softened language, full mission consent or a completed search. Mud-induced tears with actual satisfaction C4P0425–0441 remain adverse to an automatic distress brake. The Gen03 sufficient tear-brake rule remains retired, and its falsifying evidence remains load-bearing. |
| Aqua: capacity, readiness and comparison | PRESERVE the decomposition. Blocked patient access C1P0308–0346, actual buffs C1P0478–0479/C2P0515, successful purification C2P0675–0677, failed then repaired cure logistics C3P0416–0443 and reported resurrection C4P0017 have different causes. | Neither poor task information nor moral nonexecution is raw magical inability. Comparative clarity still requires a genuinely matched opportunity; Serena's new corpse-puppetry explanation cannot become earlier V14 knowledge. |
| Aqua: useful recognition, status and owned goals | STRENGTHEN the importance of belonging and renewed urgency for the long-established home goal: exclusion/neededness C1P0365–0468; heavenly recruitment demand C4P0176–0188; varied persuasion and actual departure letter. | A valued service role can be sincere alongside vanity. No two later recognition-conditioned work opportunities are manufactured; irritation alone is not the frozen direct-status-plus-prior-assignment trigger. |
| Aqua: chosen limit with Wiz | STRENGTHEN an observed relational/moral inhibition: expected opposition changes to trust, consent, love and retreat without casting FP0119–0168. | Conscience is narrator-labelled and watching eyes coexist. No uniquely pure private cause, universal response to praise or new power failure follows. Her desired return and wish to be found can coexist. |
| Megumin: identity and practical externalities | STRENGTHEN status sensitivity and retain competing friend-help: reserve classification, paper-plane disposal, known shared-document requirement, later blast, offered excuse and flinch C4P0467–0520. | Full literal Gen04-M01 eligibility and its separate ordinary-cost branch require their own audit; a general competence challenge cannot be silently exchanged for explicit denigration/replacement/imitation of Explosion. Postcast catch/refuel is not a prior exit plan. |
| Megumin: intimacy, care and limits | STRENGTHEN person- and event-specific agency: distinguishes altered shamelessness C3P0319; chosen display/claim P0370–0405; practical holding/hugging P0412–0443; ends his extended hug P0464–0466. | Affection is not universal consent. Wanting to reclaim him and liking some altered boldness can coexist; successful cure does not erase its first failed handoff. |
| Darkness: protection with mixed motives | STRENGTHEN feasible defensive contribution C3P0343–0351; PRESERVE finite endurance and partial knowledge. Trusted institutional help C2P0064–0085 and civic killing offer C3P0498–0501 add distinct domains. | Duty can recruit real cost while pleasure remains. Reflected pinching P0524–0528 is deliberate reward-seeking rather than uniformly task-oriented restraint. No single moral adjective explains all of it. |
| Darkness: privacy and standing | PRESERVE audience/meaning sensitivity with OPEN exact predecessor-condition coverage. Public intimacy disclosure C2P0087–0115 and later guarantee refusal P0373 establish a real applied local limit. | Her reaction cannot alone establish an independently expressed earlier privacy condition for Gen04-D02. Later affection, bodily enjoyment or stopped resistance cannot retroactively authorize the imposition. No genuinely incompatible continued-party/civic-duty choice is invented. |
| Yunyun: inclusion and new social stakes | STRENGTHEN useful-role participation: question about crime, trusted unique request, acceptance and invisibility C2P0217–0236; new town-defense wish C4P0483–0493. | Social anxiety does not imply poor magic or total friendlessness. The silence spell is requested but not separately staged; actual town defense and civic release remain unknown. |
| Serena: local antagonist mechanism | NEW bounded profile from this volume's evidence: persuasive counseling and control, social-mask failures, expensive dependent, doctrinal/shared-faith trap and renewed lethal action. | One-volume density supports this causal account; it does not justify unrestricted cross-context or longitudinal certainty, nor an invisible master plan. |
| Wiz / Vanir | STRENGTHEN distinct domain judgment and attachment: real but poorly matched merchandise FP0081–0114; hospitality, home, contract/name protest, shielding, chosen consent and step aside P0119–0168. | Respect, trust and self-interest coexist. The exact private reasons for the step aside remain inferred; no supplemental account is opened to fill them. |
| Chris/Eris / Iris | PRESERVE prior evidence and uncertainty. Eris actually comforts and relays institutional information C4P0006–0188; Iris is referred to, not directly observed in a new matching contrast. | Useful service does not manufacture the cumulative-burden trigger; references and absence do not confirm Iris forecasts. No secondary promotion is made merely to fill a deliverable list. |
| Ensemble | STRENGTHEN distributed capacity and local coordination: buffs/recovery/legal handoff C1P0478–0486, stealth C2P0217–0260, capture and corrected cure C3P0343–0443, later resurrection/reset/handoff. | Separate deployment improvement, provider adequacy for the actual compared task, and continuing Kazuma dependence. An adequate restoration-phase arrangement need not solve the whole officer/city problem. |

The compact explanatory gain is interaction among goals, access, state and relationship. Familiarity makes some injuries more intolerable, supplies leverage for humiliation, predicts an excuse before it is used and makes the absence of one noisy member emotionally costly. Material rewards matter, but they do not exhaust the value of a home. Status matters, but some status defense is useful task performance rather than abandonment. Technical success matters, but it can preserve a cruel goal. These are causal tensions to carry into checkpoint review, with disagreement and missing tests retained rather than patched by one exception per scene.

### Evidence weights for the new claim families

Every route below belongs to MAIN-15's admitted principal prose. H1–H4 describe how comic evidence supports reconstruction; they do not replace the separate distinction between shown action, dialogue, diary/report and inferred motive. A serious directly narrated event need not be relabelled a joke. Fantasy-domain effects can be literal events in this world without becoming human capacities in transfer. Confidence is high for a clear local act, lower for its exclusive cause or generalization; these are qualitative judgments, not estimated probabilities.

| Claim family and route | Evidence class and confidence | Transition / limit carried to the ledgers |
|---|---|---|
| Kazuma's tool search and invalidating feedback, C2P0177–0214; gratitude capture P0745–0767 | H1 local preparation, result and subsequent revision; HIGH for their order, MODERATE for general robustness. | STRENGTHEN asymmetric inference; preserve vigilance failure. Neither cleverness nor later success establishes moral or comprehensive practical competence. |
| Kazuma's controlled-state bargaining, C2P0757–0834; C3P0006–0286 | Actual impairment/order and later live acts are direct evidence; diary events retain report status. H2 for amplified appetites and harassment intensity; MODERATE for the surviving-inference/changed-goal decomposition. | OPEN the new state-specific account. Do not assert that an earlier accepted model held either the empty-puppet or wholly free-agent alternative. No secret counterplan or universal state exemption. |
| Particular-person care and self-protective presentation, C1P0350–0486; FP0172–0354; EpP0047–0065 | H1 actual care choices, offer and intention; H2 denial/insult amplification. HIGH local behavior, MODERATE mixed-cause and cross-context reach. | STRENGTHEN particular attachment with real contrary choices retained. Making Aqua cry C4P0441 remains adverse to a sufficient tear brake; that rule is retired, its failure preserved. |
| Aqua's power versus task information, PrP0008–0019; C2P0675–0690; C3P0416–0443 | H1 fictional-domain effect and actual logistical outcomes; H2 argumentative amplification. HIGH for execution/order; MODERATE for general task-alignment claims. | PRESERVE capacity/readiness/access/result separation. No known stock-preservation assignment is invented; no matched clarity comparison is manufactured. |
| Aqua's stop before Wiz, FP0119–0168 | H1 chosen nonexecution and physical retreat; comic role reversal does not negate the event. HIGH for no cast, MODERATE for conscience/trust/audience contributions. | STRENGTHEN a bounded relational/moral inhibition. Neither pure private altruism nor raw inability is established; consent, gaze and narrator attribution remain distinct. |
| Megumin's standing, document externality and support for Yunyun, C4P0467–0520 | H1 listing, voiced inference, known need, destruction and later excuse; H2 scale of retaliatory display. HIGH for order/cost knowledge, MODERATE for motive mixture and transfer. | STRENGTHEN identity sensitivity without inventing the full M01 trigger. Yunyun infers the advanced-magic reason; no completed civic release or prior carry contract follows. |
| Megumin's selected intimacy and cure role, C3P0319–0466 | H1 chosen display, actual restraint/recovery and ending the hug; H2 provocative intensity. HIGH for distinct actors/choices, MODERATE for general relationship script. | PRESERVE event-specific consent and mixed care/possessiveness. The rescuers' first logistics fail; Kazuma is their resisting target. |
| Darkness's protection, discretion and pain rewards, C2P0064–0115; C3P0343–0351/P0498–0528 | H1 protective/intervention choices and applied limit; H2 pain-seeking intensity. HIGH local acts; MODERATE causal/transfer generalization. | STRENGTHEN protection while preserving purposeful harm and finite knowledge. No universal control-loss aversion or privacy antecedent is inferred solely from the scored objection. |
| Yunyun's useful inclusion and town ties, C2P0217–0241; C4P0483–0512 | H1 request/acceptance/cast and expressed wish; H2 loneliness exaggeration. HIGH local participation, MODERATE social mechanism. | STRENGTHEN competence despite anxiety. Her friends and duty matter; Silence execution and actual defense remain unobserved. |
| Serena, Wiz and Vanir's institutional/relational constraints, C3P0063–0068; FP0081–0168 | H1 actual work/choice and explicit speech with report/inference status retained; H2 merchant/servant extremes; isolated comic labels are H3, not stable diagnostic traits. HIGH for local acts, MODERATE for motives, LOW for unobserved legal or universal rules. | OPEN bounded supporting-subject accounts. Card disclosure constrains adventurer requests, not all lawful work. Money-taking is not certified voluntary donation; step-aside motives and weather history remain attributed. |
| Distributed capture/cure/reset, C3P0343–0548; C4P0017/P0156–0165 | H1 actual task contributions, failed handoff, repair, death and later completion; HIGH sequence, MODERATE portability of organization mechanism. | STRENGTHEN local complementarity. Scope alternative-provider adequacy to the independently specified task; do not erase casualties, resource limits or future institutional uncertainty. |

No new durable claim is established from H4 nonliteral comic license alone. H2 retains the underlying disposition and the actual canonical event while limiting automatic transfer of its intensity; it is not a way to delete inconvenient cruelty or failed predictions. The corresponding ledger entries retain finer locators and epistemic qualifications, and section 14 separately governs unchanged-forecast adjudication.

## 14. All25 unchanged Gen04 commitments: V15-local branch proposals

Every Gen04 statement remains the exact prospective commitment frozen after V12. Its original25 Outcome and25 Adjudication fields remain blank; the following are new V15-local records in a postexchange receiving proposal. V13-local K03, V14-local A02 and historical Gen03-K04 falsifications remain unchanged. A single local contrary direction is distinguished from repeated-pattern or whole-tranche retirement. The initial producer memo and every postexchange field delta remain preserved. Root must still accept or revise these proposals through an attributable receiving successor; no consensus or volume acceptance is implied.

The record below retains every branch, primary chronology, strongest contrary or adjacent candidate, missing conjunct and failure diagnosis. It includes the generous pragmatic-request reading for K02, completed-revenge reconciliation for K03, assignment/challenge/inhibition alternatives for A02, state-specific agency for K04, prior recovery planning for M03, and feasible return/recruiter benefit for E02. No added personal-cost floor, physical-harm-only condition, goal-completion exemption, universal external-assigner condition or named-spell-debate prerequisite governs the old forecasts.

### MG04-K01 — CONFIRMED

- **indirect_search:** Supported by the benefit/control-rule investigation and asymmetric level-reset solution.
- **exact_capability_based_revision:** Supported at a bounded level: actual indirect-coffee harm at C2P0203–0206 invalidates the indirect-injury assumption, he explicitly generalizes reflection at210–211 and declines the contemplated explosive-potion method212. The observed change is withdrawal/restriction of that method, not proof every subsequent anecdote was invented after this test.
- **fitted_delegation:** Supported for explicit buff/heal/police/crowd assignments; distinguish assigned actions from the later reported treatment and arrest.
- **recovery_and_ethics:** Recovery preparation is present in C1, but Kazuma’s later death, lost levels, incomplete target inhibition and coercive exposure remain actual costs and failures of broader sufficiency.

**C1P0395–0401,0412–0452,0478–0505; C2P0037–0038:** Before action, Kazuma rejects an unverified curse-removal gamble and doubts ordinary Bind against a dark priest. Serena then supplies a bounded solo-interference exception. He requests support and assigns healing, police transport and crowd management before attacking. His anticipated reflected injury occurs; the next chapter reports recovery/treatment and arrest. This is fitted preparation, not a safe or ethically adequate plan merely because he is revived/healed.

**C2P0203–0212,0315–0356,0602–0638:** The cup attack causes actual shared burns C2P0203–0205 and Kazuma calls it another failure206. He explicitly learns indirect injury also reflects210–211 and states that otherwise he would have bought explosive potions212, then relocates214. This supplies a real new constraint and withdrawal of that route, without claiming an earlier executed bombing or a firm purchase intention. The later loan maneuver fails to retrieve Vanir’s contract, while money taken from a follower and renewed favor supply observed rule transitions. C2P0630–0638 remains his inference.

**C3P0484–0520,0530–0548; C4P0017–0021,0156–0161:** He rejects direct and indirect killing, faces the inadequacy of simply releasing Serena, and uses a carried level-reset potion against the unequal costs of the same reset. He anticipates his own loss before acting, warns Darkness against lethal assault, but fails to inhibit Serena’s restored Death capability in time. Actual resetting and intelligence handoff are later established; his solo claim does not erase others’ capture, cure, revival and restraint.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** The many correct plans are not evidence that every first plan adapts successfully. C2P0608 is a failed contract maneuver; C3P0543–0548 exposes a newly restored enemy capability before Kazuma and Darkness can stop it. C4P0017 reports outside recovery. These remain adverse to a general complete-planner claim even while supporting indirect construction.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The relevant capability-based revision is the actual restriction of the indirect-injury route after invalidating feedback, including rejection of an explosive extension. No firm earlier purchase plan or exact date for every prank is invented. Search and fitted delegation are independently observed; performed buffs differ from inferred unconscious treatment and unshown assigned handoffs.

**Diagnosis:** CONFIRMED for bounded local search/revision/delegation, revising the initial PARTIAL after root and semantic attention to the explicit C2P0210–0212 limitation. Recovery/ethical failure and Serena’s restored-power attack remain; this is not a complete-planner claim.


### MG04-K02 — PARTIAL

- **trusted_concrete_request_before_help:** Explicit King requests exist, and Aqua’s C1P0468 question can pragmatically request particular reassurance. No grammatical imperative or request naming combat is required. Whether that question entrusts a concrete preventable-harm responsibility rather than asks for an appraisal remains the strongest local eligibility uncertainty.
- **actual_substantive_help:** Strong adjacent evidence exists, including costly intervention and care; it does not manufacture its initiating request.

**C1P0374–0392,0427–0479,0486–0505:** Familiarity, Aqua’s visible exclusion, Megumin’s trust, Darkness’s permission and Aqua’s question about being needed precede Kazuma’s costly intervention. Aqua asks whether she is an unnecessary goddess. This can pragmatically request concrete reassurance/support; no named protective act or grammatical imperative is required. Kazuma’s meaningful buff assignment and intervention support the generous request/emotional-harm reading, while the exact entrusted responsibility and refusal consequence remain uncertain.

**FP0175–0180,0183–0201,0311–0324:** He independently brings food after learning Aqua has withdrawn and later considers limited ways to help her return. Her small wish to go home is explicitly not directed at him or a demand at313–314. There is a feasible meal/limited planning contribution, but no source-established concrete request before either choice.

**EpP0047–0065; FP0324:** Her letter states a dangerous goal; Kazuma stands, then jumps from bed intending immediate pursuit before the erased request is discovered at62. The earlier explicit risk of bodily annihilation makes danger real. The erased “please find me” is a concrete trusted-person request, but is learned after the stated help decision. The source closes without showing a subsequent search execution.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** C1P0468 is a trusted direct self-worth question after visible exclusion, so it must be assessed as a possible concrete request for reassurance/support, not rejected solely because it does not say “fight Serena.” Kazuma immediately entrusts meaningful support to her and accepts reflected injury; on a pragmatic request/emotional-harm reading this supports the forecast. The exact entrusted harm-prevention task and refusal consequence remain partly inferred. Explicit King/leveling requests C4P0152/0168/0185/0206–0220 and FP0031 are also real: his refusals/comfort preference remain contrary if the particular-person preventable-harm conjunction is judged complete. Feasible contacts/indirect help cannot be denied merely because he cannot win a solo duel.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The forecast has no physical-harm-only rule, costly-help minimum, grammatical-imperative requirement or demand that the request specify the tactic used. Strongest generous reading: C1P0468 requests reassurance about valued standing, refusal leaves ongoing emotional injury, and Kazuma’s fitted support request plus intervention supplies substantive help. Strongest limiting reading: the source establishes a concrete appraisal question but no unambiguous entrusted protective responsibility/refusal consequence. Later explicit King requests establish a request and systemic danger, but particular-person harm under refusing this contribution is still uncertain. FP0313–0314 explicitly undirected wish and EpP0062 after-decision timing remain exact limits.

**Diagnosis:** PARTIAL proposal, changing the initial NOT_TESTED because that text understated the genuine pragmatic request/emotional-harm candidate and the explicit King requests. No clean abandonment failure is asserted, and no forced full confirmation follows. Root should adjudicate this literal eligibility dispute with both readings visible.


### MG04-K03 — FALSIFIED

- **ordered_payback_expansion:** One bounded diary sequence supplies a newly learned grievance, visibly active payback accounting and an actual next act accepting known reflected pain.
- **success_and_retirement_limits:** Other successes do not automatically qualify; strongest unchanged-scope candidates are retained with their exact salience limits.
- **historical_adverse:** V13 MG04-K03 FALSIFIED remains unchanged; this local positive does not cancel it.
- **local_contrary_direction:** C2P0705–0733 supplies concrete apparent success, explicit privately active revenge satisfaction and actual scope contraction/reconciliation. Retain as a single local contrary direction, not whole-tranche retirement.

**C1P0412,0497–0505 → C2P0700–0733:** Prior selected scope is personally obstructing Serena/countering her treatment of Aqua, with a declared tomorrow interference campaign. AtC2P0705 Serena acknowledges underestimating him and announces abandoning the town. Kazuma explicitly says settling peacefully suits him714 and privately acknowledges revenge for Aqua is in a sense complete715, before asking for reconciliation724, accepting promised withdrawal725–727 and canceling mutual debts733. Reward salience is independent and before the contraction; unchanged scope cannot be converted into missing reward. Goal completion is not an added exemption. Wiz is already scorched700 and the fight continues707, so the original known-person-harm/severe-danger modifier is a genuine competing account fixed before the decision. His expressed private appraisal nevertheless stresses completed revenge and relief, not an explicitly adopted new rescue objective.

**C1P0033/0047–0051 → P0073–0077 → P0080–0091:** An initially wary/polite baseline changes after Serena credits him with the army’s losses: visible pride75 and the private “I have become that important”76 precede gloating77. This is bounded verbal/taunting expansion. The new alarming recruitment explanation begins80 and cannot be moved before77 to exempt the response. Existing risk and later caution still limit generality.

**C3P0034–0049:** Before the new robot revelation, Serena’s punch already establishes the same-site injury reflection at35–37. Kazuma then learns her responsibility for the robot rampage at44–45, explicitly construes it as harm caused to him and a large debt at46–47, and chooses a compensatory punch at48. Both crouch in pain at49. The next accepted personal exposure expands from the prior compensation regime to a retaliatory blow despite known feedback. The manageable grievance opportunity and payback appraisal are explicit before that choice; no post-act emotion is used to invent them.

**C2P0264–0288,0297–0356:** He continues preparing harassment after seeing Serena weaken, then meets a new explicit lethal threat and numerical disadvantage at285–309. The improvised exposure tactic and public call are real escalation. However, the original obstruction campaign already includes escalation and the new grave threat is independently present; this is not an unqualified clean reward-only test.

**C3P0532–0548; C4P0158–0165,0271–0277:** At532 he already plans a comfortable slow rebuild. Serena’s “most dangerous” recognition at535 is followed by his overestimate/retirement reply537; Death becomes explicit only later543, so it cannot retrospectively excuse the earlier unchanged scope. However, concrete personal enjoyment of that recognition is not independently established before537. Likewise he rejects extrapolating successive wins into a King campaign atC4P277 while Megumin and Darkness are excited. Their reward salience is not automatically his.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** The initial producer memo omitted the strongest unchanged-scope case C2P0705–0733; that omission is now explicitly repaired after root’s independently recorded question and a direct primary reread. Kazuma acknowledges completed revenge before choosing settlement, under concrete apparent victory feedback. C2P0700’s already observed harm to Wiz and ongoing conflict remain the strongest original-modifier counterreading; they are neither ignored nor treated as automatic erasure when his stated next-choice reasons are relief and completion. C3P0535–0537 retirement remains another candidate with uncertain active personal reward; later Death cannot retroactively supply its danger cue.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The C2 matched candidate has independent reward salience and a clear next contraction. The original modifier is present as competing context; whether it causally accounts for caution is underdetermined. This FALSIFIED proposal records the adverse local direction without claiming a repeated unmodified pattern or retiring the whole tendency. Positive C1 gloating and C3 robot-payback choices remain separate and cannot cancel it; V13 local F remains unchanged. The original known-person-harm/danger modifier does not require an expressly adopted new rescue goal; lack of such a declaration does not erase the already observed modifier.

**Diagnosis:** FALSIFIED, scoped to a single local contrary direction. Changed from initial CONFIRMED because a previously omitted source-established reward → reconciliation sequence is load-bearing. No net-vote treatment, goal-completion exemption or adverse-history deletion; root may retain modifier uncertainty in final adjudication rather than force a whole-tranche rejection.


### MG04-K04 — PARTIAL

- **quiet_A:** NOT_TESTED: no single new case combines an offered attractive substitute, already acknowledged particular commitment, explicit low/reduced immediate appetite, and the actual choice.
- **aroused_B:** PARTIAL/agency-qualified candidate, not a clean complete-conjunction confirmation. The prior compensation/legality account and explicit excitement are real, but knowledge of an effective feasible stop under actual moral-restraint removal remains uncertain. The original forecast does not require a normal state; the unresolved ingredient is known effective stop/agency at the scored choice.
- **noticed_tears:** Continuations and exploitation of tears remain adverse to any broader automatic brake; original Gen03-K04 FALSIFIED stays exact.

**C3P0010–0023,0034–0040; C2P0813–0815,0892–0897:** The diary order supplies a pre-existing service/compensation role. Kazuma himself elects an erotic appendix: at11–15 he establishes a payment and labels prospective harassment “legal”; excitement is explicit at16, before Serena’s refusal21. His next response22 demands a large price for stopping, and he savors her nearly crying face23. The obscene appendix is his own optional addition rather than a compelled literal diary requirement, supplying a feasible stop. Previous payment rules also give him a known possible control-release route, which he deliberately avoids. The helper/debt account is already available before this continuation; it is not inferred solely from an excuse afterward.

**C2P0846,0914–0929,0937–0955:** His earlier unsolicited breast-support offer is rejected at846; he later initiates and continues groping before her hand removes his at921. He supplies the debt-preservation account at928 and cites the earlier discarded-morals order at946 after a new shared-room objection945. The enacted soft-breast gratification is explicit919; however, the most direct price/exculpation wording at928 follows the shown groping continuation. These are strong boundary violations without pretending every B chronology is fully established.

**C3P0061–0065,0263–0286,0464–0466; EpP0004–0018:** He exploits remembered companions as leverage until Serena actually cries64, resists a task she wants even with pain263, and she later runs with flowing tears286. In Ep he is already awake, initiates a hug, performs sleep before Darkness’s protest, and falsely blames her afterward. The sleep frame is prior to the protest, but immediate arousal/intimate reward is not separately explicit enough to infer the whole B conjunction merely from hugging. Later cessation of her resistance does not retroactively consent to the initial act. His post-cure demand for more hugging464 ends when Megumin peels him away466; it does not prove autonomous restraint.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** C1P0122–0124 contains a tempting offer and refusal but visible fidgeting, not explicit reduced appetite. C4P0046–0050 is Kazuma’s own flirtation with Eris, her correction and an explicit denial of his jealousy theory; it is not an attractive substitute offered to him. FP0355–0357 immediate rejection of Aqua’s sexual warning neither supplies an offered substitute nor the full commitment/low-arousal conjunction.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** No universal altered-state exclusion is added: actual chosen tariff-writing, selective refusal and painful resistance show surviving initiative. However, optional literary content alone does not prove he knows an efficacious stop for the relevant disinhibited urge. Control-release payment efficacy is explicitly disputed atC2P0892–0898; the cure is discarded, and C3P0404 states lack of self-control. The strongest B candidates therefore preserve known/unknown agency rather than treating ordinary free choice as established. No clean A, no clean autonomous contrary B, and no restored Gen03 tear brake.

**Diagnosis:** PARTIAL, with initial B support reduced to a literal/agency-qualified candidate after independent source-only reviews. Existing harassment and actual-tear continuations remain acts regardless of the narrower test. C3P0064→0066,0105→0106–0107 and0161/0177–0181 strengthen the broad adverse record; C4P0441 shows satisfaction at having made Aqua cry, not a newly shown continuation after a separate noticed-tear cue.


### MG04-A01 — PARTIAL

- **capacity:** Supported in bounded engaged purification, support and resurrection tasks.
- **readiness:** Variable and separately observed; access blocked, sleep/distraction, unknown item function and self-selected withdrawal are not domain incapacity.
- **comparative_goal_clarity:** NOT_TESTED as a clean matched causal comparison with domain/access/resources and relevant knowledge held sufficiently comparable.

**C1P0310–0315,0478–0479; C2P0515,0547,0675–0677:** Aqua approaches an injured patient but is blocked before casting; no failed Aqua healing is shown. Support magic is actually supplied before Kazuma’s dropkick and later in the chase, with Serena recognizing its quality547. **C3P0390–0443; C4P0017,0053,0129–0140:** The special antidote is an external supplied item, not proof of Aqua’s intrinsic anti-puppet spell mastery. Her catching and pouring it after changed restraint is real restoration work. Eris reports actual resurrection work atC4P0017; revival is shown atP0124. C2P0038’s earlier unconscious healing remains Kazuma’s inference. No root or reviewer observation route for Eris is invented.

**C4P0209–0223; FP0035–0053:** Aqua proposes bounded barrier work and distinguishes remaining maintainer counts from her own capacity, with Kazuma correcting headcount. These are plans/capacity claims, not actual barrier removal in this volume. No invented spell execution is counted.

C3’s bottle transfer includes a catch/face-orientation difficulty, not unerring handling. Her embarrassment/withdrawal FP175–208 and low-confidence admission183 remain readiness/goal evidence. None can be erased by successful resurrection.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The comparative clarity branch lacks a matched clear versus unclear arrangement with sufficient independent controls. Earlier not knowing the potion function and later learning it changes relevant knowledge as well as alignment. Capacity, required-time readiness and comparative clarity remain three different results.

**Diagnosis:** PARTIAL for the compound: engaged established-domain ability is supported; practical system anticipation and guaranteed activation are not. No undefined curse or failed uncast spell is invented.


### MG04-A02 — AMBIGUOUS

- **prestige_response:** Direct challenge → rival-directed escalation is observed.
- **ongoing_assignment_displacement:** C2 escape has paused while a self-selected water service is completed; neither a completed water act nor an unassigned stock-preservation rule automatically supplies the required ongoing task. The wider escape reading remains possible, not established merely from the fight.
- **historical_adverse:** V14 A02 FALSIFIED for its scoped single local direction remains unchanged; no favorable V15 act cancels it.
- **local_contrary_candidate:** FP0119–0129: self-adopted purify-Wiz/return goal, direct rival provocation, actual ignoring of Vanir and continued original purpose. Whether the violent-goddess provocation is the exact divinity/superiority/public-recognition trigger and whether bodily interposition/cost warning inhibit the distinct contest remain explicit disputed predicates.

**FP0119–0129,0136–0168:** Before challenge Aqua explicitly adopts purifying Wiz as divine work at119–121. Her prior return-home objective and the last-officer/barrier reasoning are already established in C4P0176–0188 and FP0041–0060; the first express connection of killing this friend to her return, and her stated friend-killing pain, occurs at129 after the initial ignore128. Vanir interposes125, calls her a violent goddess, warns about repair cost126 and actively provokes127. Aqua expressly ignores him128 and continues addressing Wiz to accomplish the original task129. This is a real contrary direction under the self-adopted-assignment/direct-superiority-provocation reading. Interposition blocks access to Wiz, not obviously a prestige fight with Vanir standing in front of her; it must not be assumed to prevent all retaliation. She acknowledges friend-killing pain129, but that acknowledgment follows the initial ignore128 and cannot automatically be backdated. Later consent/trust/gaze/conscience and retreat are a distinct decision, not the earlier status outcome.

**C2P0515–0553,0564–0584,0675–0712:** Before the contest Aqua is fleeing alongside Kazuma, contributes support, and participates in attempted escape. The shopping pause does not itself prove the dangerous situation has safely ended. She then completes an independently visible hydration service for Wiz at676, before Vanir’s toilet-goddess insult689. Her response690 escalates mockery of his competence; the beam694/Reflect695 exchange continues into a sustained fight707/710–712; Wiz is seen scorched700 during that fight, while the exact causal route is Kazuma’s hedged attribution. Thus the wider practical escape/protection objective is displaced and real friendly costs follow; the already completed hydration act alone cannot be named an ongoing displaced assignment.

**FP0064–0081:** Mask-pulling is already underway before Kazuma tells her to stop so they can talk at71; she obeys72 and asks about Wiz73. Vanir disparages the gods74; she retorts76, partly self-deprecating, and Vanir proposes a fight77. Wiz’s arrival81 interrupts. The visible response is not a completed renewed Aqua attack; do not give Vanir’s challenge her agency.

**FP0119–0168,0183–0197:** Her attempted Wiz-purification plan has return-home stakes. Wiz’s trust/affection, Vanir’s contract concern and everyone’s gaze produce Aqua’s withdrawal. This is not an independently preceding direct prestige challenge and therefore does not by itself confirm or falsify A02.

**C1P0308–0350:** A patient names Aqua and she approaches; others block treatment before casting. After Serena’s treatment leaves residual wounds337, the patient rejects Aqua’s hand341–345 and she returns dejected350. If this is a direct public-recognition challenge to her service role, task-conserving/non-escalatory response is adverse; if a refusal of already externally blocked treatment, full trigger/access remains uncertain. No grave-risk exemption is manufactured from small residual wounds.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** Initial PARTIAL overstated the escape-to-fight interpretation while omitting FP0126–0129’s autonomous-looking persistence and C1’s rejected healer. These are now fully retained. A02 does not require an external assigner; self-adopted work is eligible on its actual merits. Conversely no one assigned stock preservation, Aqua says she did not know the proposed sale690, and prior “go home” was rejected before the fight. FP0074–0081 has a real challenge and relative reply, but Vanir’s duel invitation and Wiz’s arrival do not show Aqua completing a new attack or autonomously restoring the task.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** Assignment/constraint, exact status-challenge subtype and inhibition versus autonomous persistence remain consequential to whether FP0128 is a full adverse test and whether C2 has a positive displacement. These are genuine eligibility disputes, not an exemption because the outcome is contrary or because a self-chosen goal lacks an external director. New source qualifies the causal route of scorched Wiz atC2P0700 as Kazuma’s inference during an actual fight.

**Diagnosis:** AMBIGUOUS local proposal: strongest full-reading countercase explicitly preserved, with exact trigger/inhibition uncertainty. If root accepts FP0126 as a full direct superiority/divinity challenge without preventing inhibition, the local direction should be FALSIFIED with single-case scope; if not, it remains a documented adjacent adverse case rather than a hidden exception. V14 scoped F is immutable either way.


### MG04-A03 — NOT_TESTED

- **actual_useful_service:** Multiple useful acts occur, including support, resurrection and the antidote delivery.
- **recognition_before_two_new_same_service_opportunities:** Not established.
- **voluntary_ownership:** Shown locally in care/service but not enough to supply the missing temporal series.

Wiz thanks Aqua after the water conversion, but at least two separately identified further opportunities for that same useful output with aligned recognition/resources are not shown afterward.

A gifted item, one cure sequence, several steps within one service, or friendship praise is not work-specific acknowledgment followed by two further same-service opportunities.

**Contrary/adjacent evidence:** Later embarrassment, withdrawal and eating alone cannot falsify this forecast without the required recognition-before-two-opportunities structure. Equally, multiple spells across the novel cannot be pooled into one invented owned repetition series.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The complete temporal conjunction is absent: same service, actual work-specific acknowledgment first, then two distinct renewed opportunities with need/resources/alignment still available. A long offstage interval does not fill it.

**Diagnosis:** NOT_TESTED; persistent care and recognition causality remain different propositions. Ownership, affection and material rewards remain rival sustainers, not uniquely identified causes.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** Missing low-valued repeated maintenance plus concretely available attractive high-output shortcut and absence of a salient learned/external constraint before choice.

**Diagnosis:** NOT_TESTED; retain the actual damaging simplification without claiming the specific maintenance mechanism has gained validation.

The literal question is whether this explicitly denigrates/replaces/imitates Explosion in a way Megumin treats as her specialist-identity challenge. Her response and known document-sharing cost cannot alone invent the missing antecedent; they remain real identity/care evidence.

**Missing conjunctions and scope:** The uncertain/missing item is the literal denigration/replacement/imitation of Explosion, not an added demand for a named rival spell. First disposal475 precedes the explicit shared-document need476; actual destruction498–501 follows known cost and Yunyun’s civic dilemma492. Her broad specialist pride and mixed help do not erase either timing.

**Diagnosis:** NOT_TESTED proposal for the exact original trigger; source-supported general identity defense remains adjacent. A generous reading that reserve selection replaces her chosen spell with advanced-magic users must remain available for root receiving, while general role-fit criticism cannot automatically become explicit Explosion denigration.
