## 15. Hypotheses to carry into adversarial checkpoint review

No new prospective generation is frozen in this volume. The following are explicitly retrospective V15-derived questions for the V13–V15 checkpoint. They may inform new forecasts only after the unchanged Gen04 tranche is fully adjudicated; they cannot change its original triggers, modifiers, counterpart requirements, outcomes or formal failure thresholds.

1. **Reward extends an active goal only under some appraisals.** Compare recognized success that supplies a new tempting opportunity with success read as sufficient completion. V14's changed timetable/credit staging, V13's unchanged prior hunt and V15's relief/truce all matter. The old forecast did not contain a goal-completion exemption. A successor may test a newly explicit distinction prospectively, but cannot use it to erase the older countercase. Preserve original danger and personally known harm cues before explaining any difference.
2. **Agency has several components.** Under imposed allegiance, test attention, motor control, inhibition, belief, calculation, reward and choice separately. Retained bargaining is compatible with coercion; favorable enemy defeat is compatible with no intended resistance plan. A transferred model must specify which components its new world's influence actually changes rather than importing magical puppetry whole.
3. **Aqua's status response may recruit either argument or demonstrated service.** Fix the actual assignment, constraint and kind of challenge first. Her known capacity, willingness, access and task information must remain separate. V14's contrary case is not rescued by treating every practical response as hidden escalation; V15's already-purified stock does not supply a prior stock-preservation assignment after the fact.
4. **Relational loss has several meanings.** Wanting heavenly return, regretting a forced arrival, wishing not to be left and accepting eventual separation can coexist. Test concrete feasibility, duration, instrumental benefit and available contact, rather than equating every departure with durable membership loss. Kazuma's imagined material benefits and subsequent boredom/approach remain contrary appraisals inside the same person.
5. **Megumin can help a friend through a solution that also repairs her own standing.** The letter episode supplies mixed motives and a known ordinary externality; it does not isolate a pure helping mechanism or every exact specialty-trigger condition. Future tests must distinguish general rank criticism, explicit Explosion replacement and an independently accepted grave contribution burden. Do not infer a prior carry agreement from a successful later catch.
6. **Darkness's local boundary is about audience/meaning rather than all lost control.** Record what she sought, who unexpectedly appears, the preference stated before the response and whether equal standing is explicitly threatened. Her real complaint and later applied guarantor refusal are evidence, while enjoyment remains evidence too. Neither can cancel the other by stereotype.
7. **Organization is phase-specific.** A team can repair a failed cure without Kazuma directing it, yet remain unable to complete the surrounding neutralization safely. Compare the same actual roles, resources, targets and handoffs before claiming either universal dependence on him or universal replaceability.
8. **Quiet and urgent care need different language tests.** The entering J01 already specifies ordinary questions/offers/disclosure for quiet care and task-specific command/resource/handoff language for independently new urgent care. Preserve every fixed V15 role/window/exclusion, including already-active care, sparse speech and adjacent roles. Do not convert a pursuit intention into an uttered command or change a specialist mapping after reading its forms.

The checkpoint must combine all three volumes, retain every tested and untested branch and explain failures with source evidence. The humor distribution needs its complete fixed three-volume denominator, including unresolved and soft-scoped units; local or preparatory arithmetic is not the pooled verdict. Only then may a dated Gen05 model and applicable V16–V17 forecasts be frozen and published. V16 remains unopened until that separate publication gate closes.

Three further producer-derived probes preserve useful distinctions without becoming new Gen04 conditions:

| New prospective comparison only | Variables to record before choice | Discriminating adverse observation |
|---|---|---|
| Particular care versus defensive presentation | Actual trusted request, preventable harm, feasible options, personal costs and likely credit as separate variables. Neither a grammatical imperative nor a large-cost threshold is added to Gen04. | Repeated literal entrusted-need cases receive no meaningful contribution/search/alternative chiefly for comfort, even where another unrequested care scene is favorable. |
| Knowledge carried into the next equivalent choice | Newly learned mechanism, visibility of the next cue, ongoing attention and any changed state before applying a safeguard. | Correct inference repeatedly fails to govern a comparable available next choice; a later victory cannot erase that earlier matched integration failure. |
| Whole comic mechanism versus nested form | Complete source-first eligibility/bounds, short written/narrator gags, cultural context and independently assigned primary dependence. | Excluding brief form cases, changing boundaries after classification or treating cultural unfamiliarity as automatic L3 invalidates the result. |

## 17. Confidence, exposure limits and still-open work

High confidence applies to exact source identity, admitted component boundaries, actual shown choices/casts, the order of the failed and repaired cure, controlled-state changes, known summons externality before the blast, and the distinction between completed reset/police handoff and later planned events. Confidence about motives is proportional to access: Kazuma's stated current thoughts are evidence about his reported appraisal; another person's expression, explanation or letter is not omniscient access to that person's mind. His hostile descriptions can preserve true events while distorting credit or value.

The substantive reading uses the complete admitted principal narrative. Root, the continuing producer, continuing independent discovery reader and semantic reviewer each completed its own ordered principal scope; their overlaps are not summed into more source coverage or more independent discoveries. Root's separate ruby-pair projection inspection covers all 2,513 base/reading pairs, without claiming every XML serialization was visually inspected. Discovery and later source-focused receiving checks have distinct scopes. No acoustic performance, voice-actor intention, adaptation, translation, wiki or fandom evidence is admitted.

Both original humor discoveries sealed before uncoded exchange. The fixed 150-unit register retains every one of the 289 original objects and their source-scoped flags, including all 36 soft-touched units. The 95 voice records retain their original speakers, recipients, role mappings, before/after windows and limits. Shared primary text, shared factual QA and collaborative boundary decisions prevent a claim of full blindness, human inter-rater reliability or statistically independent trials. The 66 same-speaker after-window presentation groups are receiving aids. Both complete formal assignments are now sealed and comparison has been released; sections 7–8 and the paired registers preserve identities, genuine disagreements and uncertainty bounds. The initial source-only section did not certify those future events; this final receiving successor records their actual completion while leaving analytical acceptance and publication pending.

Model knowledge of a familiar series cannot establish a new test as absolutely blind. The supported process claim is narrower: exact frozen entering statements, documented admitted exposure order, separately complete source-first discovery and separately sealed full formal assignment files before outcome exchange, followed by an independently sealed producer all 25 memo before predicate exchange. Exact root releases are 2026-10-17T05:20:39.514588Z and 05:22:09.844424Z. Shared factual QA, uncoded reconciliation and actual postseal review access are disclosed in section 1; no later or excluded source was consulted. V15's new explanation of the cemetery is not written back into V14's evidentiary state. Any later expanded result must retain source class, admission phase and analyst exposure chronology. No model-tier label, separate human expert or undisclosed fresh reader is claimed.

Important unresolved matters include the exact cause of each character's mixed motives, control's contribution to a normally available inhibition, literal forecast eligibility where conjuncts are absent, the adequacy of an alternative organizer for a whole surrounding problem, actual heavenly-return feasibility, the fate of village/town defense, royal transfer, source-specific animal ontology, the unnamed threatened body interference and its degree of completion, actual departure escort and the unfinished pursuit. Absence of a scene is not evidence of a successful prediction. Attractive interpretations that require later material remain hypotheses.

V15 does not close the project or create Gen05. After complete substantive receiving acceptance, its reading, ten ledger appends and synchronized canonical map/source records need one exact-path publication transaction, remote byte readback, source audit, housekeeping and final exact-head Repository integration audit success. Then the separate V13–V15 checkpoint must adjudicate every unchanged Gen04 forecast, complete the pooled humor/voice work and freeze/publish Gen05 before V16 exposure. The final main-series checkpoint, specialist syntheses, reconstruction/simulation suite and immutable main-only freeze precede all four holdouts. Expanded prose, edition bonuses, five mixed/reference objects, post-validation models, full synthesis, indexes and final audit remain later mandatory responsibilities. The 41-object identity inventory is not literary completion.

These are completed analytical passes on one admitted corpus, not a claim of twelve separate full literary readings. The final review package has17 sections and ten separate append payloads; all25 local statuses remain attributable receiving proposals until root’s adjudication. Complete source reading, complete coded comparison, analytical acceptance and publication are distinct events.
